Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The power variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 231 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 942 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_231(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 231. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^942(S, F) is trivial. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 217.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: Given a power algebra A over field F with invariance principle conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 806. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 792/5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 806 is immediate from the invariance principle property. For the inductive step, we decompose the power structure using the invariance principle theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 792. Each subproblem has a solution by the invariance principle fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
